slates daemon: the watchdog beats every 100 ms and finds a shard stuck after a 1000 ms window without a step, with no CPU progress and no wait for a CPU, or with a window's CPU run (the window is the liveness budget); a shard waiting for a CPU is starved, not stuck, and the beat goes on; an anchor reads the beat, so a stuck shard withholds it

slates anchor: a starting daemon is killed once it makes no CPU progress for the 1000 ms liveness window, or one of its threads runs that much CPU without its first beat, in its own CPU time (read from the OS, each thread's)
slates daemon: the watchdog beats every 100 ms and finds a shard stuck after a 1000 ms window without a step, with no CPU progress and no wait for a CPU, or with a window's CPU run (the window is the liveness budget); a shard waiting for a CPU is starved, not stuck, and the beat goes on; an anchor reads the beat, so a stuck shard withholds it
